<script type="text/javascript">
                        $(document).ready(function () {
                            $('#txtSearchAjaxBox').keyup(function (e) {

                                var keyword = $('#txtSearchAjaxBox').val();
                                keyword = keyword.replace(/'/g, '').replace(/\\/g, '');

                                if (keyword.length >= 3) {

                                    $('#Loading').show();
                                    $('#LoadingFix').hide();

                                    ajdata = "{'search_keyword':'" + keyword + "'}";

                                    $.ajax({

                                        type: "POST",

                                        url: "/Default.aspx/SearchKeyword",

                                        data: ajdata,

                                        contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",

                                        dataType: "json",

                                        success: function (result) {
                                            $("#SearchResult").empty();
                                            for (var i = 0; i < result.d.length; i++) {
                                                $("#SearchResult").append("<div class='ResultSearchRow'><a style='color:#434343' target='_blank' href='/" +
                                                    result.d[i].En_Url + "_/" + "'><div style='float: right;'><img style='border: 1px solid rgb(204, 204, 204); border-radius: 50px;' width='40px' height='40px' src='/Image/Softwares/Icon/" +
                                                    result.d[i].IconImage + "' alt='" + result.d[i].Title + "' /></div>" +
                                                    "<div style='text-align: left; position: relative; top: 0;font-size: 10pt;'>" + result.d[i].Title + "</div>" +
                                                    "<div style='text-align: left; position: relative; top: 0;font-size: 9pt'>" + result.d[i].Summary + "</div></a></div>");
                                            }

                                            $("#SearchResult").fadeIn();
                                            $('#Loading').hide();
                                            $('#LoadingFix').show();
                                        },
                                        error: function (xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
                                            alert(xhr.responseText);
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                                else {
                                    $("#SearchResult").fadeOut();
                                    $('#Loading').hide();
                                    $('#LoadingFix').show();
                                }


                            });

                            $(document).click(function (event) {
                                if (!$(event.target).closest('#SearchResult').length) {
                                    if ($('#SearchResult').is(":visible")) {
                                        $('#SearchResult').hide()
                                    }
                                }
                            })

                            $(document).keyup(function (e) {
                                if (e.keyCode == 27) {
                                    $('#SearchResult').hide(); // esc
                                    $('#txtSearchAjaxBox').val('');
                                }
                            });

                        });


                    </script>
